No parent or place is to be cleared without an evidenced replacement.

## Alexander and Nancy Glasgow — 29 September 2026

New original evidence and competing parentage tests are synchronized locally. John Glasgow, farmer, remains Alexander's probable father; no family-link upgrade is proposed. Nancy's contemporary death notice confirms 31 October 1891 at Plattsburgh, adds the Peristrome funeral and reports about 75, conflicting with her will's age. Complete additive drafts preserve the captured live biographies and citations:

- [Glasgow-951](../research/Glasgow-951/research-update-2026-09-29.wiki): notice, age uncertainty, Glenleslie namesake test and a clearly provisional Scottish Thompson family candidate.
- [Glasgow-4070](../research/Glasgow-4070/research-update-2026-09-29.wiki): notice and original docket chronology.

- [Glasgow-4093](../research/Glasgow-4093/research-update-2026-09-29.wiki): indexed Scottish candidate and comparison with the unproved Tannybrake widow. The blank live birth date stays blank.

**Pending live amendments; no live WikiTree writes made in this review.** Earlier completion history is retained in the queue.

## GRONI births, marriages and deaths — 23 September 2026

The [152-row death audit](indexes/groni-death-match-audit-2026-09-23.csv) has been cross-checked against 307 supplied births, 102 marriages and live WikiTree duplicate searches. Eighty entries now fit live profiles: 52 pre-existing profiles and 28 created from reviewed index rows. Their local findings, canonical IDs and public audit links are synchronized; individual catalogue dossiers remain pending a public WikiTree metadata capture. The newly recovered [Anne (Cushnehan) Glasgow](https://www.wikitree.com/wiki/Cushnehan-1) already cites the same 1877 death-register entry 162 and needs no edit; [Glasgow-3475](https://www.wikitree.com/wiki/Glasgow-3475), [Glasgow-3444](https://www.wikitree.com/wiki/Glasgow-3444), [McGowan-3525](https://www.wikitree.com/wiki/McGowan-3525), and [Glasgow-3889](https://www.wikitree.com/wiki/Glasgow-3889) now carry the reviewed exact death dates.

Twenty-one rows have possible profiles. The newly identified [Rose Ann Glasgow (Glasgow-1422)](https://www.wikitree.com/wiki/Glasgow-1422) is a possible fit for Rosetta's 1890 Ballymena death, while [Margaret (Irwin) Glasgow (Unknown-766238)](https://www.wikitree.com/wiki/Unknown-766238) has the exact day of the 1867 Coleraine death in her fields but a biography that explicitly says the death is unproved. Neither death is assigned here. Seventy-two rows have no suitable fit after the local and live name, date and locality review; 17 have a single compatible birth entry in the supplied list, which is a lead rather than identity proof. Three tentative marriage-and-birth family clusters cover seven of those deaths, including two distinct infant Sarahs in Belfast. The corrected live `searchPerson` calls worked without rate-limit errors; the earlier failure was an invalid API call. A no-fit row still needs a distinct-person assessment before profile creation. Five Robert entries are assigned elsewhere and two death entries lack a captured forename. See the [full-return priorities](indexes/groni-full-record-priorities-2026-09-23.json) for targeted brick-wall work.

## Deep free-space evidence audit — 29 August 2026

The manual review covered all 71 saved pages and 219 machine-ranked passages.
The [full conclusions](../research/free-space-pages/deep-missing-evidence-analysis.md)
separate bio-ready facts from same-name, AI/OCR and relationship speculation.

### Second-pass corrections — 29 August 2026

The independent contradiction and markup pass found that the two John-1022
research pages were materially stale. Their saved master copies now use
Glenleslie, County Antrim, withdraw the unsupported Robert-1143 parentage and
James-1144 half-brother claims, rule out the Killycurragh framework, and assess
Glasgow-3474 as the strongest but still unproved nephew candidate. Five other
saved pages received markup-only repairs; no people, locations or source links
were removed.

are not assigned to a profile merely by name.

do not establish Maria Lacey, a mortgage, Cape Town residence or a commercial

from Alexander's own adult court appearance. Keep Eliza Glasgow's predeceased
husband in `TAB MHG 913/43` separate pending her death notice.

## Prescott, Canada West cluster — 13 August 2026

[Samuel Glasgow (Glasgow-4010), the Prescott merchant](https://www.wikitree.com/wiki/Glasgow-4010), his uncertain wife and four record-defined children were created on 19 August 2026. The locally reported shipbuilder father remains unattached pending an original kinship record.

[Robert Glasgow, Prescott shipbuilder](research/Glasgow-4150/1842_Canada_West_Prescott_Robert_Glasgow.md)
is **CREATION-READY**. His registered will on FamilySearch film 008200419,
image 35, directly names brothers Daniel, Samuel, John and Thomas, deceased
brother James's children, and sister Mary; [[Glasgow-4010|Samuel]] is his
brother, not his son. Ireland is a defensible uncertain creation birthplace
from Samuel's original 1851 census birthplace, not a found birth record for
Robert.

## New-profile decisions

These identity decisions are ranked separately and do not displace the top 20
existing-profile corrections. A documentary fact is consolidated into a compatible existing
profile wherever possible; under-identified records are routed to supporting pages rather than
forced into person profiles. A new profile is appropriate only where the evidence distinguishes a person and no compatible profile survives
the duplicate audit. Project-index and duplicate searches were refreshed against the
local One Tree export on **6 August 2026**, including `Glasgow`, `Glascow`,
`Glasco` and `Glascho` variants.

| Priority | Person established by evidence | Why this addition matters | Draft |
| ---: | --- | --- | --- |
| 3 | [Maria Glasgow (Glasgow-3928)](https://www.wikitree.com/wiki/Glasgow-3928), born 22 September 1800, and [Maria (Glasgow) Wallace (Glasgow-2137)](https://www.wikitree.com/wiki/Glasgow-2137) | **READY — PROBABLE MERGE into Glasgow-3928.** The Bible proves the natal identity; the Hiltner database uses the same person for Alexander Wallace's wife, with compatible chronology, locality and daughter-name evidence. Preserve the derivative-evidence qualification after merging. | [Complete post-merge profile](../research/Glasgow-3928/Glasgow-3928.md) · [Identity audit](../research/Glasgow-3928/findings.md) |

[[Glasgow-3989]] lacks the Drumragh, Speir or named-child bridge and is excluded as the present match. | [Paste-ready draft](research/Glasgow-4155/1828_Ireland_Drumragh_Mary_Glasgow.md) — **CREATION-READY** |

## Complete findings audit

The queue is not limited to the ranked table above. Every profile with a saved
`findings.md` has been audited and is listed here so no research is lost. The
linked file is the complete handoff: apply its specific relationship, vital,
identity, source, or biography changes unless the profile is marked complete in
the applied-profile audit above. A finding that only says “retain as uncertain”
is intentionally non-detachment guidance, not a request to remove the person.

## Catalogue creation-ready batch — 18 September 2026

The 62 creation-ready "No confirmed WikiTree link" identities were created live
(57 new profiles, Glasgow-4098 and Glasgow-4099–4155) from their reviewed drafts,
with a duplicate double-check per draft. The 1577 Hirmenschelis "[Androw?]" draft
was skipped because that child is read as Marjory (existing Glasgow-3589), and a
Helen Glasgow duplicate (Glasgow-4117) was merged into Glasgow-4098.

Their record-named parents were created and attached (father + mother per parish
cluster, e.g. John Glasgow-4156 and Isabell Kelle-79 of Irvine; James Glasgow-4157
and Helen Nasmith-38 of Irvine; Hugh Glasgow-4166 and Margaret McGechan-24 of
Kilmarnock; Robert Glasgow-4168 and Katherine Watson-53558 of Libberton), each
with an uncertain parish location and a birth "before {child year − 19}". Spouse
links follow where the record names one (e.g. John Glasgow-4152 of Saltcoats to
Isobell Gray).

### Quality cleanup of the created batch — 18 September 2026

Following creation, the 57 record profiles and their created parents/spouses were
audited against three quality requirements: explicit date-status rationale and
uncertain locations, natural record-based prose, and profile/source interlinking.

- **Child profiles rewritten and field-corrected (live, verified):** Glasgow-4098,
  4099, 4100–4116, 4119–4121, 4123–4137, 4139–4140, 4142–4152, 4154–4155.
  Baptism-derived births now use the baptism date with status `before`; recorded
  birth dates use `certain`; every inferred parish location is marked uncertain.
  The AI audit boilerplate ("no compatible candidate", "result ID(s)", "next
  test") was removed and each biography now states why the year and place were set.
- **Held:** Glasgow-4122 — its father was merged into Glasgow-1139 (structured
  birth 1721) while Sarah was baptised in 1726, producing an impossible parent
  age. The chronology warning was not bypassed; the merge needs review.
- **Parent/spouse biographies rewritten:** Glasgow-4157, 4158, 4164–4171,
  4175–4178 and Rae-3515, McGechan-24, Giffen-664, Watson-53558, Gibson-37713,
  Auld-2246, Smith-407061, Mitchell-58830 (applied live from `/tmp/fixes 3`).
- **Merges reported by the duplicate sweep (not all validated):** Glasgow-4156 → Glasgow-2475,
  Glasgow-4159 → Glasgow-4100, Glasgow-4161 → Glasgow-4158, Glasgow-4162 →
  Glasgow-3479, Glasgow-4163 → Glasgow-1139, Glasgow-4172 → Glasgow-3582,
  Glasgow-4173 → Glasgow-1105, Glasgow-4174 → Glasgow-3586, Glasgow-4118 →
  Glasgow-3478, Kelle-79 → Kyle-2471, Nasmith-38 → Nasmith-37, Rae-3514 →
  Rae-3513, Borland-1621 → Borland-1479, Ferguson-29462 → Ferguson-15168,
  Morison-997 → Morrison-24595, Daston-13 → Eastone-3, Glasgow-4117 → Glasgow-4098.
- **Audit qualification, 21 September:** 4163→1139 was a known wrong merge. The same-child rationale for 4118→3478 remains unproved: compare the 1736/1738 originals and the September/October 1738 birth readings before any further identity change.
- **Resolved (same day, as reported):** Saltcoats father attachment (Glasgow-4152 attached as
  father of 4151/4153); Cleora Glasgow-4148 attached to father Hiram Glasgow-3995,
  mother Mary Ann Brandon Kennedy (new Kennedy-35691) and husband Leland McNary
  Speers (new Speers-583, married 17 Oct 1867); Mary Glasgow-4155 given spouse
  William Speir (new Speir-488), inferred as the other parent of the bride in the
  1828 Kilrea marriage entry; that entry is not their own marriage record. Glasgow-4122
  was re-parented from the wrongly merged son (Glasgow-1139) to the adult John
  Glasgow MA (Glasgow-1077), the wrong spouse link on 1139 was removed, and both
  biographies were rewritten. The 4163→1139 merge is recorded as wrong in
  `research/glasgow-record-batch-2026-09-18/findings.md`.
- **Draft retirement:** the 57 created-profile drafts were moved into
  `research/<WikiTree-ID>/` with inbound links updated.
- **Still open:** a profile for Rev. Leonidas K. Glasgow; catalogue integration
  of this batch.

### Larne joining-generation candidate profiled — 18 September 2026

The 1769 Larne marriage-record man "John³ Glasgow", identified from Donna Jean
Glasgow's Kilwaughter/Larne chronology, was created live as
[[Glasgow-4179|John Glasgow]] (born about 1747, Larne uncertain) with his wife
[[Allan-6611|Rose Allan]] (born about 1750, County Antrim uncertain; Allan-8249 merged here 21 September), married
1769, both cited from the chronology transcription. At creation no parents were attached; on 21 September Hugh-1499 was connected as an uncertain father, with no mother, as a low-confidence hypothesis. The chronology names no father. He remains an unconnected candidate for the
Larne-side joining generation of the Rasharkin/Lisnagaver branch; the Desertoghill
father link is noted in the profiles and findings but not attached. See
`research/Glasgow-951/larne-reconstruction-2026-09-13.md`.

## Findmypast page 3 and Abraham HOLD — 22 October 2026

Twenty additional transcripts are recorded under existing people or qualified candidates. Search coverage remains INCOMPLETE: pages 1–3 contain 60 inspected results, plus one supporting OPR reinspection. Page 3 adds no new person or parent attachment. The earlier Abraham 1786 creation recommendation is withdrawn: indexed birth year is not independent evidence excluding Glasgow-1227 or Glasgow-1235. His complete held account retains both candidates and the original citations.

[Current record assignments and unresolved questions](/branches/early-scottish-records/#chapter-findmypast-born-before-1800). Candidate child deaths, adult-baptism possibilities and the Ohio death-year discrepancy remain research comparisons; no speculative vital replacement is queued.
